Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geophysics operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 277. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 228 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the geophysics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^277 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 228-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the geophysics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 247. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
